nw14_monthly = {
if = { limit = { tag = GER NOT = { has_country_flag = nw_parliament_active } } remove_ideas = nw14_deadlock }
if = { limit = { tag = GER has_country_flag = nw_parliament_active } if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = nw_ger_settled } } add_to_variable = { var = nw_tension value = 2 } clamp_variable = { var = nw_tension min = 0 max = 100 } } if = { limit = { check_variable = { nw_tension > 69 } } if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = nw14_deadlock } } add_ideas = nw14_deadlock } } else = { remove_ideas = nw14_deadlock } }
if = { limit = { tag = GER has_country_flag = nw_network_income } add_to_variable = { var = nw_network value = 4 } clamp_variable = { var = nw_network min = 0 max = 100 } }
if = { limit = { tag = SOV } if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = nw_alexei_dead } NOT = { has_country_flag = nw_rus_recovered } } add_to_variable = { var = nw_health value = -1 } clamp_variable = { var = nw_health min = 0 max = 100 } } if = { limit = { check_variable = { nw_trust < 25 } } if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = nw14_provincial_distrust } } add_ideas = nw14_provincial_distrust } } else = { remove_ideas = nw14_provincial_distrust } }
if = { limit = { tag = AUS } if = { limit = { check_variable = { nw_federal_trust < 30 } } if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = nw14_federal_deadlock } } add_ideas = nw14_federal_deadlock } } else = { remove_ideas = nw14_federal_deadlock } }
if = { limit = { tag = HUN } if = { limit = { check_variable = { nw_hungarian_trust < 25 } } if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = nw14_hungarian_deadlock } } add_ideas = nw14_hungarian_deadlock } } else = { remove_ideas = nw14_hungarian_deadlock } }
if = { limit = { has_country_flag = nw14_fragment has_war = no } set_rule = { can_join_factions = yes can_create_factions = yes } clr_country_flag = nw14_fragment }

}
